# Cyclone UI (Light High Contrast) Tokens

Use these documented design-token names and values as the source of truth; do not invent replacement values.

## Blur

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `blur.2xl` | `dimension` | `40px` | 2X large blur (40px) |
| `blur.3xl` | `dimension` | `64px` | 3X large blur (64px) |
| `blur.lg` | `dimension` | `16px` | Large blur (16px) |
| `blur.md` | `dimension` | `12px` | Medium blur (12px) |
| `blur.sm` | `dimension` | `8px` | Small blur (8px) |
| `blur.xl` | `dimension` | `24px` | Extra large blur (24px) |
| `blur.xs` | `dimension` | `4px` | Extra small blur (4px) |

## Border Radius

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `border-radius.2xl` | `dimension` | `1rem` | 2X large radius (1rem) |
| `border-radius.3xl` | `dimension` | `1.5rem` | 3X large radius (1.5rem) |
| `border-radius.4xl` | `dimension` | `2rem` | 4X large radius (2rem) |
| `border-radius.button` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for triggers and other pressable elements (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Button, Badge, DatePicker triggers, SearchInput results, Select items, Tabs triggers and CodeBlock header controls. Circular and rounded Button variants override it. |
| `border-radius.card` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for cards and card-like tiles (6px, `border-radius.md`). Slightly tighter than `container` so cards nested inside a container still read as inset. Used by Card and FilePicker. |
| `border-radius.checkbox` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-sm)` | Corner radius for the checkbox box (6px, `border-radius.md`). Kept smaller than `control` so the small box still reads as a square rather than a pill. Used by Checkbox. |
| `border-radius.container` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for large framed regions that group other content (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Container, Accordion, Callout, CodeBlock, FileTree, FilePicker, Table, Stepper and InlineCodeText. |
| `border-radius.control` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for form controls that accept input (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Matches `button` so inputs and buttons line up when placed side by side. Used by Input, TextArea, Select, Field, RadioGroup and Rating. |
| `border-radius.dialog` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-2xl)` | Corner radius for modal dialogs (12px, `border-radius.xl`). Larger than other surfaces to set modal content apart from the page beneath it. Used by Dialog. |
| `border-radius.drawer` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-4xl)` | Corner radius for drawers (32px, `border-radius.4xl`). The large rounding marks a panel that slides over the page from an edge. Used as the default `borderRadius` of Drawer. |
| `border-radius.full` | `dimension` | `100%` | Full radius (100%) |
| `border-radius.lg` | `dimension` | `0.5rem` | Large radius (0.5rem) |
| `border-radius.md` | `dimension` | `0.375rem` | Medium radius (0.375rem) |
| `border-radius.popover` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for popovers and dropdown menus (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Popover and the Select dropdown list. |
| `border-radius.sheet` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-zero)` | Corner radius for sheets (0px, `border-radius.zero`). Sheets slide in flush against a viewport edge, so their corners stay square. Applied by Sheet to its exposed corners. |
| `border-radius.sm` | `dimension` | `0.25rem` | Small radius (0.25rem) |
| `border-radius.tooltip` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for tooltips (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Tooltip. |
| `border-radius.trigger` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for triggers interfaces (6px, `border-radius.sm`). Used by Tabs (indicators). |
| `border-radius.xl` | `dimension` | `0.75rem` | Extra large radius (0.75rem) |
| `border-radius.xs` | `dimension` | `0.125rem` | Extra small radius (0.125rem) |
| `border-radius.zero` | `dimension` | `0rem` | No radius |

## Color

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `color.backdrop` | `color` | `#00000066` | Semi-transparent color that dims the page behind modal content and softens floating shadows. Used as the backdrop of Dialog and Sheet, and as the shadow color for Popover, Tooltip, the Select dropdown and the `floating` Container. |
| `color.base` | `color` | `var(--color-neutral-13)` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. |
| `color.base-accent` | `color` | `var(--color-base)` | Alias of base for the base theme |
| `color.base-active` | `color` | `#17171b` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. (active, 10% lighter) |
| `color.base-disabled` | `color` | `#151519` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. (disabled, 85% saturation, 35% over oklch(0.3839 0.0045 286.26)) |
| `color.base-inactive` | `color` | `#000000` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. (inactive, 40% darker) |
| `color.base-subtle` | `color` | `var(--color-neutral-5)` | Low-emphasis neutral color for the `base` theme, a softer alternative to the full accent. Exposed as `muted`; used for `subtle` Button fills, Badge hover, Switch tracks, Footer and `secondary` Container backgrounds, FileTree row hover and selected DataTable rows. |
| `color.base-subtle-active` | `color` | `#b9b9c0` | Low-emphasis neutral color for the `base` theme, a softer alternative to the full accent. Exposed as `muted`; used for `subtle` Button fills, Badge hover, Switch tracks, Footer and `secondary` Container backgrounds, FileTree row hover and selected DataTable rows. (active, 12% darker) |
| `color.base-subtle-disabled` | `color` | `#676776` | Low-emphasis neutral color for the `base` theme, a softer alternative to the full accent. Exposed as `muted`; used for `subtle` Button fills, Badge hover, Switch tracks, Footer and `secondary` Container backgrounds, FileTree row hover and selected DataTable rows. (disabled, 85% saturation, 35% over oklch(0.3839 0.0045 286.26)) |
| `color.base-subtle-inactive` | `color` | `#000000` | Low-emphasis neutral color for the `base` theme, a softer alternative to the full accent. Exposed as `muted`; used for `subtle` Button fills, Badge hover, Switch tracks, Footer and `secondary` Container backgrounds, FileTree row hover and selected DataTable rows. (inactive, 40% darker) |
| `color.black` | `color` | `#000000` | Near-black neutral. |
| `color.blue.1` | `color` | `#f8fbff` | Pale periwinkle blue. |
| `color.blue.2` | `color` | `#cbdeff` | Light periwinkle blue. |
| `color.blue.3` | `color` | `#9ec1ff` | Light sky blue. |
| `color.blue.4` | `color` | `#70a4ff` | Soft cornflower blue. |
| `color.blue.5` | `color` | `#4387ff` | Bright cobalt blue. |
| `color.blue.6` | `color` | `#025eff` | Medium azure blue. |
| `color.blue.7` | `color` | `#0145be` | Deep azure blue. |
| `color.blue.8` | `color` | `#012e7c` | Vivid cerulean blue. |
| `color.blue.9` | `color` | `#00163b` | Rich cerulean blue. |
| `color.border` | `color` | `var(--color-neutral-6)` | Low-contrast color for borders, dividers and separators. Use it to outline controls and split content without adding visual weight. Default color of Divider, and the border color for Input, TextArea, Select, Checkbox, RadioGroup, Switch, Accordion, Table, Tabs and Footer, and the Sheet drag handle. |
| `color.border-active` | `color` | `#000000` | Low-contrast color for borders, dividers and separators. Use it to outline controls and split content without adding visual weight. Default color of Divider, and the border color for Input, TextArea, Select, Checkbox, RadioGroup, Switch, Accordion, Table, Tabs and Footer, and the Sheet drag handle. (active, light contrast primitive) |
| `color.border-disabled` | `color` | `#5a5a66` | Low-contrast color for borders, dividers and separators. Use it to outline controls and split content without adding visual weight. Default color of Divider, and the border color for Input, TextArea, Select, Checkbox, RadioGroup, Switch, Accordion, Table, Tabs and Footer, and the Sheet drag handle. (disabled, 85% saturation, 35% over oklch(0.3839 0.0045 286.26)) |
| `color.border-inactive` | `color` | `#000000` | Low-contrast color for borders, dividers and separators. Use it to outline controls and split content without adding visual weight. Default color of Divider, and the border color for Input, TextArea, Select, Checkbox, RadioGroup, Switch, Accordion, Table, Tabs and Footer, and the Sheet drag handle. (inactive, 40% darker) |
| `color.danger` | `color` | `var(--color-red-7)` | Danger accent used by the `danger` theme. Use it for destructive actions, such as delete buttons, and for error states. Applied by Alert `danger` and `error` Messages and ValidationText. |
| `color.danger-accent` | `color` | `var(--color-danger)` | Alias of danger for the danger theme |
| `color.danger-active` | `color` | `#27000c` | Danger accent used by the `danger` theme. Use it for destructive actions, such as delete buttons, and for error states. Applied by Alert `danger` and `error` Messages and ValidationText. (active, 25% darker) |

Applied by Alert `danger` and `error` Messages and ValidationText. (disabled, 85% saturation, 35% over oklch(0.3839 0.0045 286.26)) |

Use it for the primary mark of a neutral series, such as lines, bars, points and legend swatches. Suits baselines, totals and comparison series that should not compete with colored data. |

## Typography

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `typography.body-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Default body text style: Storm Sans regular at 18px (`font-size.md`) with normal line height. Use it for paragraphs, descriptions and most running copy. BodyText uses it by default, as do the components built on BodyText. |
| `typography.body-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Smaller body text style: Storm Sans medium at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with normal line height. Use it for secondary copy in dense layouts, such as table rows, sidebars and card metadata, where `body-md` would feel too large. |
| `typography.caption` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}","fontStyle":"italic"}` | Caption style: Storm Sans regular italic at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for helper text, annotations and small supporting labels. Used by Field helper and validation text, ValidationText, EyebrowText, Slider value labels and the CodeBlock header file name. |
| `typography.code` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Monospace code style: Google Sans Code regular at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for source code, file paths, identifiers and other literal values. Used by CodeBlock, InlineCodeText, FileTree, Stepper and code content inside Accordion. |
| `typography.display-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest display style: Storm Sans semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.display-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large display style: Storm Sans semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.display-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium display style: Storm Sans bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |
| `typography.editorial-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.editorial-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.editorial-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium editorial display style: Storm Serif bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |

Use it for the short kicker or category label that sits directly above a heading. Used by EyebrowText. |

Use it for form labels, column headers and other short labels that sit above content. Used by LabelText (resting label), DataTable column headings and TypeTable column labels. |

Use it for compact labels that must stay out of the way of their content. LabelText switches to it when `floating` is set, such as a field label that has floated up into the input border. |

# Cyclone UI (Light Dimmed) Tokens

Use these documented design-token names and values as the source of truth; do not invent replacement values.

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `blur.2xl` | `dimension` | `40px` | 2X large blur (40px) |
| `blur.3xl` | `dimension` | `64px` | 3X large blur (64px) |
| `blur.lg` | `dimension` | `16px` | Large blur (16px) |
| `blur.md` | `dimension` | `12px` | Medium blur (12px) |
| `blur.sm` | `dimension` | `8px` | Small blur (8px) |
| `blur.xl` | `dimension` | `24px` | Extra large blur (24px) |
| `blur.xs` | `dimension` | `4px` | Extra small blur (4px) |

## Border Radius

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `border-radius.2xl` | `dimension` | `1rem` | 2X large radius (1rem) |
| `border-radius.3xl` | `dimension` | `1.5rem` | 3X large radius (1.5rem) |
| `border-radius.4xl` | `dimension` | `2rem` | 4X large radius (2rem) |
| `border-radius.button` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for triggers and other pressable elements (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Button, Badge, DatePicker triggers, SearchInput results, Select items, Tabs triggers and CodeBlock header controls. Circular and rounded Button variants override it. |
| `border-radius.card` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for cards and card-like tiles (6px, `border-radius.md`). Slightly tighter than `container` so cards nested inside a container still read as inset. Used by Card and FilePicker. |
| `border-radius.checkbox` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-sm)` | Corner radius for the checkbox box (6px, `border-radius.md`). Kept smaller than `control` so the small box still reads as a square rather than a pill. Used by Checkbox. |
| `border-radius.container` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for large framed regions that group other content (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Container, Accordion, Callout, CodeBlock, FileTree, FilePicker, Table, Stepper and InlineCodeText. |
| `border-radius.control` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for form controls that accept input (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Matches `button` so inputs and buttons line up when placed side by side. Used by Input, TextArea, Select, Field, RadioGroup and Rating. |
| `border-radius.dialog` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-2xl)` | Corner radius for modal dialogs (12px, `border-radius.xl`). Larger than other surfaces to set modal content apart from the page beneath it. Used by Dialog. |
| `border-radius.drawer` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-4xl)` | Corner radius for drawers (32px, `border-radius.4xl`). The large rounding marks a panel that slides over the page from an edge. Used as the default `borderRadius` of Drawer. |
| `border-radius.full` | `dimension` | `100%` | Full radius (100%) |
| `border-radius.lg` | `dimension` | `0.5rem` | Large radius (0.5rem) |
| `border-radius.md` | `dimension` | `0.375rem` | Medium radius (0.375rem) |
| `border-radius.popover` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for popovers and dropdown menus (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Popover and the Select dropdown list. |
| `border-radius.sheet` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-zero)` | Corner radius for sheets (0px, `border-radius.zero`). Sheets slide in flush against a viewport edge, so their corners stay square. Applied by Sheet to its exposed corners. |
| `border-radius.sm` | `dimension` | `0.25rem` | Small radius (0.25rem) |
| `border-radius.tooltip` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for tooltips (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Tooltip. |
| `border-radius.trigger` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for triggers interfaces (6px, `border-radius.sm`). Used by Tabs (indicators). |
| `border-radius.xl` | `dimension` | `0.75rem` | Extra large radius (0.75rem) |
| `border-radius.xs` | `dimension` | `0.125rem` | Extra small radius (0.125rem) |
| `border-radius.zero` | `dimension` | `0rem` | No radius |

## Color

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `color.backdrop` | `color` | `#373a3d66` | Semi-transparent color that dims the page behind modal content and softens floating shadows. Used as the backdrop of Dialog and Sheet, and as the shadow color for Popover, Tooltip, the Select dropdown and the `floating` Container. |
| `color.base` | `color` | `var(--color-neutral-13)` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. |
| `color.base-accent` | `color` | `var(--color-base)` | Alias of base for the base theme |
| `color.base-active` | `color` | `#4d4d4f` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. (active, 10% lighter) |
| `color.base-disabled` | `color` | `#4c4c4e` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. ## Drop Shadow

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `drop-shadow.2xl` | `shadow` | `0px 25px 25px 0px #00000026` | 2X large drop shadow |
| `drop-shadow.lg` | `shadow` | `0px 4px 4px 0px #00000026` | Large drop shadow |

## Typography

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `typography.body-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Default body text style: Storm Sans regular at 18px (`font-size.md`) with normal line height. Use it for paragraphs, descriptions and most running copy. BodyText uses it by default, as do the components built on BodyText. |
| `typography.body-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Smaller body text style: Storm Sans medium at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with normal line height. Use it for secondary copy in dense layouts, such as table rows, sidebars and card metadata, where `body-md` would feel too large. |
| `typography.caption` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}","fontStyle":"italic"}` | Caption style: Storm Sans regular italic at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for helper text, annotations and small supporting labels. Used by Field helper and validation text, ValidationText, EyebrowText, Slider value labels and the CodeBlock header file name. |
| `typography.code` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Monospace code style: Google Sans Code regular at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. # Cyclone UI (Light) Tokens

Use these documented design-token names and values as the source of truth; do not invent replacement values.

## Blur

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `blur.2xl` | `dimension` | `40px` | 2X large blur (40px) |
| `blur.3xl` | `dimension` | `64px` | 3X large blur (64px) |
| `blur.lg` | `dimension` | `16px` | Large blur (16px) |
| `blur.md` | `dimension` | `12px` | Medium blur (12px) |
| `blur.sm` | `dimension` | `8px` | Small blur (8px) |
| `blur.xl` | `dimension` | `24px` | Extra large blur (24px) |
| `blur.xs` | `dimension` | `4px` | Extra small blur (4px) |

## Border Radius

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `border-radius.2xl` | `dimension` | `1rem` | 2X large radius (1rem) |
| `border-radius.3xl` | `dimension` | `1.5rem` | 3X large radius (1.5rem) |
| `border-radius.4xl` | `dimension` | `2rem` | 4X large radius (2rem) |
| `border-radius.button` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for triggers and other pressable elements (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Button, Badge, DatePicker triggers, SearchInput results, Select items, Tabs triggers and CodeBlock header controls. Circular and rounded Button variants override it. |
| `border-radius.card` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for cards and card-like tiles (6px, `border-radius.md`). Slightly tighter than `container` so cards nested inside a container still read as inset. Used by Card and FilePicker. |
| `border-radius.checkbox` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-sm)` | Corner radius for the checkbox box (6px, `border-radius.md`). Kept smaller than `control` so the small box still reads as a square rather than a pill. Used by Checkbox. |
| `border-radius.container` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for large framed regions that group other content (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Container, Accordion, Callout, CodeBlock, FileTree, FilePicker, Table, Stepper and InlineCodeText. |
| `border-radius.control` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for form controls that accept input (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Matches `button` so inputs and buttons line up when placed side by side. Used by Input, TextArea, Select, Field, RadioGroup and Rating. |
| `border-radius.dialog` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-2xl)` | Corner radius for modal dialogs (12px, `border-radius.xl`). Larger than other surfaces to set modal content apart from the page beneath it. Used by Dialog. |
| `border-radius.drawer` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-4xl)` | Corner radius for drawers (32px, `border-radius.4xl`). The large rounding marks a panel that slides over the page from an edge. Used as the default `borderRadius` of Drawer. |
| `border-radius.full` | `dimension` | `100%` | Full radius (100%) |
| `border-radius.lg` | `dimension` | `0.5rem` | Large radius (0.5rem) |
| `border-radius.md` | `dimension` | `0.375rem` | Medium radius (0.375rem) |
| `border-radius.popover` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for popovers and dropdown menus (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Popover and the Select dropdown list. |
| `border-radius.sheet` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-zero)` | Corner radius for sheets (0px, `border-radius.zero`). Sheets slide in flush against a viewport edge, so their corners stay square. Applied by Sheet to its exposed corners. |
| `border-radius.sm` | `dimension` | `0.25rem` | Small radius (0.25rem) |
| `border-radius.tooltip` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for tooltips (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Tooltip. |
| `border-radius.trigger` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for triggers interfaces (6px, `border-radius.sm`). Used by Tabs (indicators). |
| `border-radius.xl` | `dimension` | `0.75rem` | Extra large radius (0.75rem) |
| `border-radius.xs` | `dimension` | `0.125rem` | Extra small radius (0.125rem) |
| `border-radius.zero` | `dimension` | `0rem` | No radius |

## Typography

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `typography.body-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Default body text style: Storm Sans regular at 18px (`font-size.md`) with normal line height. Use it for paragraphs, descriptions and most running copy. BodyText uses it by default, as do the components built on BodyText. |
| `typography.body-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Smaller body text style: Storm Sans medium at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with normal line height. Use it for secondary copy in dense layouts, such as table rows, sidebars and card metadata, where `body-md` would feel too large. |
| `typography.caption` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}","fontStyle":"italic"}` | Caption style: Storm Sans regular italic at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for helper text, annotations and small supporting labels. Used by Field helper and validation text, ValidationText, EyebrowText, Slider value labels and the CodeBlock header file name. |
| `typography.code` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Monospace code style: Google Sans Code regular at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for source code, file paths, identifiers and other literal values. Used by CodeBlock, InlineCodeText, FileTree, Stepper and code content inside Accordion. |
| `typography.display-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest display style: Storm Sans semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. | `typography.title-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium title style: Storm Sans medium at 16px (`font-size.md`) with snug line height. Use it for form labels, column headers and other short labels that sit above content. Used by LabelText (resting label), DataTable column headings and TypeTable column labels. |
| `typography.title-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Small title style: Storm Sans bold at 12px (`font-size.sm`) with snug line height. Use it for compact labels that must stay out of the way of their content. LabelText switches to it when `floating` is set, such as a field label that has floated up into the input border. |
| `typography.title-xl` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Large title style: Storm Sans semibold at 18px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for form labels, column headers and other short labels that sit above content. Used by LabelText (resting label), DataTable column headings and TypeTable column labels. |
| `typography.title-xs` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.xxs}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Extra small title style: Storm Sans medium at 10px (`font-size.xxs`) with snug line height. Use it for compact labels that must stay out of the way of their content. LabelText switches to it when `floating` is set, such as a field label that has floated up into the input border. |

## Z Index

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `z-index.0` | `number` | `0` | Base stacking level |
| `z-index.10` | `number` | `100` | Low stacking level |
| `z-index.20` | `number` | `200` | Raised stacking level |
| `z-index.30` | `number` | `300` | Elevated stacking level |
| `z-index.40` | `number` | `400` | High stacking level |
| `z-index.50` | `number` | `500` | Overlay stacking level |
| `z-index.60` | `number` | `600` | Modal stacking level |
| `z-index.70` | `number` | `700` | Popover stacking level |
| `z-index.80` | `number` | `800` | Toast stacking level |
| `z-index.90` | `number` | `900` | Topmost stacking level |
# Cyclone UI (Dark High Contrast) Tokens

Use these documented design-token names and values as the source of truth; do not invent replacement values.

## Blur

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `blur.2xl` | `dimension` | `40px` | 2X large blur (40px) |
| `blur.3xl` | `dimension` | `64px` | 3X large blur (64px) |
| `blur.lg` | `dimension` | `16px` | Large blur (16px) |
| `blur.md` | `dimension` | `12px` | Medium blur (12px) |
| `blur.sm` | `dimension` | `8px` | Small blur (8px) |
| `blur.xl` | `dimension` | `24px` | Extra large blur (24px) |
| `blur.xs` | `dimension` | `4px` | Extra small blur (4px) |

## Border Radius

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `border-radius.2xl` | `dimension` | `1rem` | 2X large radius (1rem) |
| `border-radius.3xl` | `dimension` | `1.5rem` | 3X large radius (1.5rem) |
| `border-radius.4xl` | `dimension` | `2rem` | 4X large radius (2rem) |
| `border-radius.button` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for triggers and other pressable elements (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Button, Badge, DatePicker triggers, SearchInput results, Select items, Tabs triggers and CodeBlock header controls. Circular and rounded Button variants override it. |
| `border-radius.card` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for cards and card-like tiles (6px, `border-radius.md`). Slightly tighter than `container` so cards nested inside a container still read as inset. Used by Card and FilePicker. |
| `border-radius.checkbox` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-sm)` | Corner radius for the checkbox box (6px, `border-radius.md`). Kept smaller than `control` so the small box still reads as a square rather than a pill. Used by Checkbox. |
| `border-radius.container` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for large framed regions that group other content (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Container, Accordion, Callout, CodeBlock, FileTree, FilePicker, Table, Stepper and InlineCodeText. |
| `border-radius.control` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for form controls that accept input (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Matches `button` so inputs and buttons line up when placed side by side. Used by Input, TextArea, Select, Field, RadioGroup and Rating. |
| `border-radius.dialog` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-2xl)` | Corner radius for modal dialogs (12px, `border-radius.xl`). Larger than other surfaces to set modal content apart from the page beneath it. Used by Dialog. |
| `border-radius.drawer` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-4xl)` | Corner radius for drawers (32px, `border-radius.4xl`). The large rounding marks a panel that slides over the page from an edge. Used as the default `borderRadius` of Drawer. |
| `border-radius.full` | `dimension` | `100%` | Full radius (100%) |
| `border-radius.lg` | `dimension` | `0.5rem` | Large radius (0.5rem) |
| `border-radius.md` | `dimension` | `0.375rem` | Medium radius (0.375rem) |
| `border-radius.popover` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for popovers and dropdown menus (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Popover and the Select dropdown list. |
| `border-radius.sheet` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-zero)` | Corner radius for sheets (0px, `border-radius.zero`). Sheets slide in flush against a viewport edge, so their corners stay square. Applied by Sheet to its exposed corners. |
| `border-radius.sm` | `dimension` | `0.25rem` | Small radius (0.25rem) |
| `border-radius.tooltip` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for tooltips (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Tooltip. |
| `border-radius.trigger` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for triggers interfaces (6px, `border-radius.sm`). Used by Tabs (indicators). |
| `border-radius.xl` | `dimension` | `0.75rem` | Extra large radius (0.75rem) |
| `border-radius.xs` | `dimension` | `0.125rem` | Extra small radius (0.125rem) |
| `border-radius.zero` | `dimension` | `0rem` | No radius |

## Typography

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `typography.body-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Default body text style: Storm Sans regular at 18px (`font-size.md`) with normal line height. Use it for paragraphs, descriptions and most running copy. BodyText uses it by default, as do the components built on BodyText. |
| `typography.body-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Smaller body text style: Storm Sans medium at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with normal line height. Use it for secondary copy in dense layouts, such as table rows, sidebars and card metadata, where `body-md` would feel too large. |
| `typography.caption` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}","fontStyle":"italic"}` | Caption style: Storm Sans regular italic at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for helper text, annotations and small supporting labels. Used by Field helper and validation text, ValidationText, EyebrowText, Slider value labels and the CodeBlock header file name. |
| `typography.code` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Monospace code style: Google Sans Code regular at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for source code, file paths, identifiers and other literal values. Used by CodeBlock, InlineCodeText, FileTree, Stepper and code content inside Accordion. |
| `typography.display-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest display style: Storm Sans semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.display-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large display style: Storm Sans semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.display-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium display style: Storm Sans bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |
| `typography.editorial-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.editorial-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.editorial-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium editorial display style: Storm Serif bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |
| `typography.eyebrow` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.extrabold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Eyebrow style: Google Sans Code semibold at 18px (`font-size.md`) with tight line height. Use it for the short kicker or category label that sits directly above a heading. Used by EyebrowText. |
| `typography.title-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.extrabold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Medium title style: Storm Sans bold at 16px (`font-size.md`) with snug line height. Use it for form labels, column headers and other short labels that sit above content. Used by LabelText (resting label), DataTable column headings and TypeTable column labels. |

Use it for form labels, column headers and other short labels that sit above content. Used by LabelText (resting label), DataTable column headings and TypeTable column labels. |
| `typography.title-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Small title style: Storm Sans bold at 12px (`font-size.sm`) with snug line height. Use it for compact labels that must stay out of the way of their content. LabelText switches to it when `floating` is set, such as a field label that has floated up into the input border. |

# Cyclone UI (Dark Dimmed) Tokens

Use these documented design-token names and values as the source of truth; do not invent replacement values.

## Blur

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `blur.2xl` | `dimension` | `40px` | 2X large blur (40px) |
| `blur.3xl` | `dimension` | `64px` | 3X large blur (64px) |
| `blur.lg` | `dimension` | `16px` | Large blur (16px) |
| `blur.md` | `dimension` | `12px` | Medium blur (12px) |
| `blur.sm` | `dimension` | `8px` | Small blur (8px) |
| `blur.xl` | `dimension` | `24px` | Extra large blur (24px) |
| `blur.xs` | `dimension` | `4px` | Extra small blur (4px) |

## Border Radius

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `border-radius.2xl` | `dimension` | `1rem` | 2X large radius (1rem) |
| `border-radius.3xl` | `dimension` | `1.5rem` | 3X large radius (1.5rem) |
| `border-radius.4xl` | `dimension` | `2rem` | 4X large radius (2rem) |
| `border-radius.button` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for triggers and other pressable elements (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Button, Badge, DatePicker triggers, SearchInput results, Select items, Tabs triggers and CodeBlock header controls. Circular and rounded Button variants override it. |
| `border-radius.card` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for cards and card-like tiles (6px, `border-radius.md`). Slightly tighter than `container` so cards nested inside a container still read as inset. Used by Card and FilePicker. |
| `border-radius.checkbox` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-sm)` | Corner radius for the checkbox box (6px, `border-radius.md`). Kept smaller than `control` so the small box still reads as a square rather than a pill. Used by Checkbox. |
| `border-radius.container` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for large framed regions that group other content (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Container, Accordion, Callout, CodeBlock, FileTree, FilePicker, Table, Stepper and InlineCodeText. |
| `border-radius.control` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for form controls that accept input (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Matches `button` so inputs and buttons line up when placed side by side. Used by Input, TextArea, Select, Field, RadioGroup and Rating. |
| `border-radius.dialog` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-2xl)` | Corner radius for modal dialogs (12px, `border-radius.xl`). Larger than other surfaces to set modal content apart from the page beneath it. Used by Dialog. |
| `border-radius.drawer` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-4xl)` | Corner radius for drawers (32px, `border-radius.4xl`). The large rounding marks a panel that slides over the page from an edge. Used as the default `borderRadius` of Drawer. |
| `border-radius.full` | `dimension` | `100%` | Full radius (100%) |
| `border-radius.lg` | `dimension` | `0.5rem` | Large radius (0.5rem) |
| `border-radius.md` | `dimension` | `0.375rem` | Medium radius (0.375rem) |
| `border-radius.popover` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for popovers and dropdown menus (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Popover and the Select dropdown list. |
| `border-radius.sheet` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-zero)` | Corner radius for sheets (0px, `border-radius.zero`). Sheets slide in flush against a viewport edge, so their corners stay square. Applied by Sheet to its exposed corners. |
| `border-radius.sm` | `dimension` | `0.25rem` | Small radius (0.25rem) |
| `border-radius.tooltip` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for tooltips (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Tooltip. |
| `border-radius.trigger` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for triggers interfaces (6px, `border-radius.sm`). Used by Tabs (indicators). |
| `border-radius.xl` | `dimension` | `0.75rem` | Extra large radius (0.75rem) |
| `border-radius.xs` | `dimension` | `0.125rem` | Extra small radius (0.125rem) |
| `border-radius.zero` | `dimension` | `0rem` | No radius |

## Color

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `color.backdrop` | `color` | `#35322666` | Semi-transparent color that dims the page behind modal content and softens floating shadows. Used as the backdrop of Dialog and Sheet, and as the shadow color for Popover, Tooltip, the Select dropdown and the `floating` Container. |
| `color.base` | `color` | `var(--color-neutral-2)` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. |
| `color.base-accent` | `color` | `var(--color-base)` | Alias of base for the base theme |
| `color.base-active` | `color` | `#b8b8b8` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. (active, 10% darker) |
| `color.base-disabled` | `color` | `#808081` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. Use it for primary controls and emphasized content that should not carry a brand or status color. Most components read it as `accent`, for example Button fills, the Switch thumb, Checkbox marks, LabelText, Spinner and Progress indicators. (disabled, 35% over oklch(0.3839 0.0045 286.26)) |
| `color.base-inactive` | `color` | `#808080` | Neutral, high-contrast accent used by the default `base` theme. ## Typography

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `typography.body-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Default body text style: Storm Sans regular at 18px (`font-size.md`) with normal line height. Use it for paragraphs, descriptions and most running copy. BodyText uses it by default, as do the components built on BodyText. |
| `typography.body-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Smaller body text style: Storm Sans medium at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with normal line height. Use it for secondary copy in dense layouts, such as table rows, sidebars and card metadata, where `body-md` would feel too large. |
| `typography.caption` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}","fontStyle":"italic"}` | Caption style: Storm Sans regular italic at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for helper text, annotations and small supporting labels. Used by Field helper and validation text, ValidationText, EyebrowText, Slider value labels and the CodeBlock header file name. |
| `typography.code` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Monospace code style: Google Sans Code regular at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for source code, file paths, identifiers and other literal values. Used by CodeBlock, InlineCodeText, FileTree, Stepper and code content inside Accordion. |
| `typography.display-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest display style: Storm Sans semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |

# Cyclone UI (Dark) Tokens

Use these documented design-token names and values as the source of truth; do not invent replacement values.

| `blur.3xl` | `dimension` | `64px` | 3X large blur (64px) |
| `blur.lg` | `dimension` | `16px` | Large blur (16px) |
| `blur.md` | `dimension` | `12px` | Medium blur (12px) |
| `blur.sm` | `dimension` | `8px` | Small blur (8px) |
| `blur.xl` | `dimension` | `24px` | Extra large blur (24px) |
| `blur.xs` | `dimension` | `4px` | Extra small blur (4px) |

## Border Radius

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `border-radius.2xl` | `dimension` | `1rem` | 2X large radius (1rem) |
| `border-radius.3xl` | `dimension` | `1.5rem` | 3X large radius (1.5rem) |
| `border-radius.4xl` | `dimension` | `2rem` | 4X large radius (2rem) |
| `border-radius.button` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for triggers and other pressable elements (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Button, Badge, DatePicker triggers, SearchInput results, Select items, Tabs triggers and CodeBlock header controls. Circular and rounded Button variants override it. |
| `border-radius.card` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for cards and card-like tiles (6px, `border-radius.md`). Slightly tighter than `container` so cards nested inside a container still read as inset. Used by Card and FilePicker. |
| `border-radius.checkbox` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-sm)` | Corner radius for the checkbox box (6px, `border-radius.md`). Kept smaller than `control` so the small box still reads as a square rather than a pill. Used by Checkbox. |
| `border-radius.container` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-xl)` | Corner radius for large framed regions that group other content (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Used by Container, Accordion, Callout, CodeBlock, FileTree, FilePicker, Table, Stepper and InlineCodeText. |
| `border-radius.control` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for form controls that accept input (8px, `border-radius.lg`). Matches `button` so inputs and buttons line up when placed side by side. Used by Input, TextArea, Select, Field, RadioGroup and Rating. |
| `border-radius.dialog` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-2xl)` | Corner radius for modal dialogs (12px, `border-radius.xl`). Larger than other surfaces to set modal content apart from the page beneath it. Used by Dialog. |
| `border-radius.drawer` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-4xl)` | Corner radius for drawers (32px, `border-radius.4xl`). The large rounding marks a panel that slides over the page from an edge. Used as the default `borderRadius` of Drawer. |
| `border-radius.full` | `dimension` | `100%` | Full radius (100%) |
| `border-radius.lg` | `dimension` | `0.5rem` | Large radius (0.5rem) |
| `border-radius.md` | `dimension` | `0.375rem` | Medium radius (0.375rem) |
| `border-radius.popover` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for popovers and dropdown menus (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Popover and the Select dropdown list. |
| `border-radius.sheet` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-zero)` | Corner radius for sheets (0px, `border-radius.zero`). Sheets slide in flush against a viewport edge, so their corners stay square. Applied by Sheet to its exposed corners. |
| `border-radius.sm` | `dimension` | `0.25rem` | Small radius (0.25rem) |
| `border-radius.tooltip` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for tooltips (6px, `border-radius.md`). Used by Tooltip. |
| `border-radius.trigger` | `dimension` | `var(--border-radius-lg)` | Corner radius for triggers interfaces (6px, `border-radius.sm`). Used by Tabs (indicators). |
| `border-radius.xl` | `dimension` | `0.75rem` | Extra large radius (0.75rem) |
| `border-radius.xs` | `dimension` | `0.125rem` | Extra small radius (0.125rem) |
| `border-radius.zero` | `dimension` | `0rem` | No radius |

## Typography

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `typography.body-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Default body text style: Storm Sans regular at 18px (`font-size.md`) with normal line height. Use it for paragraphs, descriptions and most running copy. BodyText uses it by default, as do the components built on BodyText. |
| `typography.body-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Smaller body text style: Storm Sans medium at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with normal line height. Use it for secondary copy in dense layouts, such as table rows, sidebars and card metadata, where `body-md` would feel too large. |
| `typography.caption` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}","fontStyle":"italic"}` | Caption style: Storm Sans regular italic at 16px (`font-size.sm`) with tight line height. Use it for helper text, annotations and small supporting labels. Used by Field helper and validation text, ValidationText, EyebrowText, Slider value labels and the CodeBlock header file name. |
| `typography.code` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.normal}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Monospace code style: Google Sans Code regular at 20px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for source code, file paths, identifiers and other literal values. Used by CodeBlock, InlineCodeText, FileTree, Stepper and code content inside Accordion. |
| `typography.display-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest display style: Storm Sans semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.display-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large display style: Storm Sans semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.display-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium display style: Storm Sans bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |
| `typography.editorial-hero` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.5xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Largest editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 60px (`font-size.5xl`) with snug line height. Reserve it for a single hero headline per page, such as a landing or marketing banner. HeadingText applies it for `level="hero"` (rendered as an h1). |
| `typography.editorial-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.3xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Large editorial display style: Storm Serif semibold at 36px (`font-size.3xl`) with snug line height. Use it for page titles and top-level section headings. HeadingText applies it for levels `1`/`title` (h1) and `2`/`lg` (h2). |
| `typography.editorial-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Serif","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.xl}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium editorial display style: Storm Serif bold at 24px (`font-size.xl`) with snug line height. Use it for section and card headings. HeadingText applies it for level `3`/`md` (h3) and as its default font. |
| `typography.eyebrow` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Google Sans Code","fontWeight":"{font-weight.extrabold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Eyebrow style: Google Sans Code semibold at 18px (`font-size.md`) with tight line height. Use it for the short kicker or category label that sits directly above a heading. Used by EyebrowText. |
| `typography.title-lg` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.extrabold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Medium title style: Storm Sans bold at 16px (`font-size.md`) with snug line height. Use it for form labels, column headers and other short labels that sit above content. Used by LabelText (resting label), DataTable column headings and TypeTable column labels. |
| `typography.title-md` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.md}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Medium title style: Storm Sans medium at 16px (`font-size.md`) with snug line height. Use it for form labels, column headers and other short labels that sit above content. Used by LabelText (resting label), DataTable column headings and TypeTable column labels. |
| `typography.title-sm` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.bold}","fontSize":"{font-size.sm}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Small title style: Storm Sans bold at 12px (`font-size.sm`) with snug line height. Use it for compact labels that must stay out of the way of their content. LabelText switches to it when `floating` is set, such as a field label that has floated up into the input border. |
| `typography.title-xl` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.semibold}","fontSize":"{font-size.lg}","lineHeight":"{line-height.tight}"}` | Large title style: Storm Sans semibold at 18px (`font-size.lg`) with snug line height. Use it for form labels, column headers and other short labels that sit above content. Used by LabelText (resting label), DataTable column headings and TypeTable column labels. |
| `typography.title-xs` | `typography` | `{"fontFamily":"Storm Sans","fontWeight":"{font-weight.medium}","fontSize":"{font-size.xxs}","lineHeight":"{line-height.snug}"}` | Extra small title style: Storm Sans medium at 10px (`font-size.xxs`) with snug line height. Use it for compact labels that must stay out of the way of their content. LabelText switches to it when `floating` is set, such as a field label that has floated up into the input border. |

## Z Index

| Token | Type | Value | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `z-index.0` | `number` | `0` | Base stacking level |
| `z-index.10` | `number` | `100` | Low stacking level |
| `z-index.20` | `number` | `200` | Raised stacking level |
| `z-index.30` | `number` | `300` | Elevated stacking level |
| `z-index.40` | `number` | `400` | High stacking level |
| `z-index.50` | `number` | `500` | Overlay stacking level |
| `z-index.60` | `number` | `600` | Modal stacking level |
| `z-index.70` | `number` | `700` | Popover stacking level |
| `z-index.80` | `number` | `800` | Toast stacking level |
| `z-index.90` | `number` | `900` | Topmost stacking level |
